Static experiment catalog

This page uses the same statuses and evidence as the interactive catalog. It works without JavaScript. A verified workflow applies to the stated inputs and scope; inspecting historical output is not a new computation.

Start here: Download the computational checkout, extract it and open a terminal in its root. Select an experiment below, follow its environment instructions, then run and verify into a new or empty output directory. Read the quickstart.

Documentation: 20261005-r19. Computational source, inputs and receipts retain their immutable r14 identities.

Environment and supported commands

Use the exact environment documented for this workflow. Activate the created environment before the commands below; python must resolve to that environment, not the system interpreter.

Environment and method instructions

Windows PowerShell: these commands call the created environment interpreter explicitly; activation is not required.

python -m venv .venv
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pip install numpy==1.24.3 scipy==1.10.1 pandas==1.5.3 networkx==3.1 shapely==2.1.2
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pip install PyYAML==6.0.3

Run computes a new result; inspect checks retained evidence. A workflow without a run action cannot be newly computed through this entry.

Run

.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -B tools/mcl_recovered.py run boston-gmns-exchange-r1 --output ../results/boston-gmns-exchange-r1-run

Verify

.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -B tools/mcl_recovered.py verify boston-gmns-exchange-r1 --run ../results/boston-gmns-exchange-r1-run

Inspect

.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -B tools/mcl_recovered.py inspect boston-gmns-exchange-r1 --output ../results/boston-gmns-exchange-r1-inspect

# Windows x86-64; requires Conda. Clean installation and both native ranks were verified.
$nativePrefix = Join-Path (Get-Location) '.mcl-runtime\native-boston'
$nativePackageCache = Join-Path $env:LOCALAPPDATA 'mcl-native-pkgs'
$previousCondaPackageCache = $env:CONDA_PKGS_DIRS
try {
    $env:CONDA_PKGS_DIRS = $nativePackageCache
    conda create --yes --prefix $nativePrefix --file environments/boston-native-win-64.lock
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Native Conda creation failed.' }
} finally {
    $env:CONDA_PKGS_DIRS = $previousCondaPackageCache
}
& (Join-Path $nativePrefix 'python.exe') -m pip install --no-deps --require-hashes -r environments/boston-native-pip.txt
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Native pip installation failed.' }
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe tools/reproduction/check_boston_native_environment.py --prefix $nativePrefix
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Native environment identity check failed.' }
$env:MCL_NATIVE_PYTHON = Join-Path $nativePrefix 'python.exe'
$env:MCL_IPOPT = Join-Path $nativePrefix 'Library\bin\ipopt.exe'

.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe tools/mcl_reproduction_check.py --experiment boston-native-l3-ranks26-52 --environment
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe tools/mcl_reproduce.py describe boston-native-l3-ranks26-52
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe tools/mcl_reproduce.py run boston-native-l3-ranks26-52 --output results/boston-native-l3-ranks26-52
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe tools/mcl_reproduce.py verify boston-native-l3-ranks26-52 --run results/boston-native-l3-ranks26-52

                                    Environment and method instructions

                                    Windows PowerShell: the controller uses the native Python 3.9 interpreter and IPOPT configured by these commands. Conda is required.

# Windows x86-64; requires Conda. Install the pinned native environment; a fresh Hong Kong native rerun is not certified by this setup text.
$nativePrefix = Join-Path (Get-Location) '.mcl-runtime\native-boston'
$nativePackageCache = Join-Path $env:LOCALAPPDATA 'mcl-native-pkgs'
$previousCondaPackageCache = $env:CONDA_PKGS_DIRS
try {
    $env:CONDA_PKGS_DIRS = $nativePackageCache
    conda create --yes --prefix $nativePrefix --file environments/boston-native-win-64.lock
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Native Conda creation failed.' }
} finally {
    $env:CONDA_PKGS_DIRS = $previousCondaPackageCache
}
& (Join-Path $nativePrefix 'python.exe') -m pip install --no-deps --require-hashes -r environments/boston-native-pip.txt
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Native pip installation failed.' }
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe tools/reproduction/check_boston_native_environment.py --prefix $nativePrefix
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Native environment identity check failed.' }
$env:MCL_NATIVE_PYTHON = Join-Path $nativePrefix 'python.exe'
$env:MCL_IPOPT = Join-Path $nativePrefix 'Library\bin\ipopt.exe'

                                    & (Join-Path $nativePrefix 'python.exe') -B tools/mcl_recovered.py run HK-H1-L3-R26 --output ../results/HK-H1-L3-R26-run --ipopt "$env:MCL_IPOPT"

                                    Inspect

                                    & (Join-Path $nativePrefix 'python.exe') -B tools/mcl_recovered.py inspect HK-H1-L3-R26 --output ../results/HK-H1-L3-R26-inspect
